    BASE_DIR = sys._MEIPASS
    
    # --- إعداد مسار GStreamer للعمل على أي جهاز كمبيوتر ---
    # 0. إضافة مجلد bin الخاص بـ GStreamer إلى متغير PATH
    # هذا ضروري للعثور على مكتبات DLL الأساسية مثل gstreamer-1.0-0.dll
    gst_bin_path = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'bin')
    if os.path.isdir(gst_bin_path):
        os.environ['PATH'] = gst_bin_path + os.pathsep + os.environ.get('PATH', '')
        print(f"GStreamer bin path added to PATH: {gst_bin_path}")
    else:
        print(f"GStreamer bin path not found at: {gst_bin_path}")

    # 1. الحصول على مسار مجلد gstreamer-1.0 الذي تم تضمينه
    gst_plugin_path = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'lib', 'gstreamer-1.0')
    
    # 2. التحقق من وجود المسار
    if os.path.isdir(gst_plugin_path):
        # 3. تعيين متغير البيئة GST_PLUGIN_PATH
        os.environ['GST_PLUGIN_PATH'] = gst_plugin_path
        print(f"GST_PLUGIN_PATH set to: {gst_plugin_path}")
    else:
        print(f"GStreamer plugin path not found at: {gst_plugin_path}")

else:
    # إذا كان التطبيق يعمل كملف بايثون عادي
    BASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))

def build_camera_name_map():
    """يبني قاموس ربط بين source واسم الكاميرا من ملف الإعدادات."""
    camera_name_map = {}
    try:
        if os.path.exists(CONFIG_FILE):
            with open(CONFIG_FILE, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                cameras = json.load(f)
                for cam in cameras:
                    src = str(cam.get('source', '')).strip()
                    name = cam.get('name', src)
                    if src:
                        camera_name_map[src] = name
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"خطأ في بناء قاموس أسماء الكاميرات: {e}")
    return camera_name_map

CAMERA_NAME_MAP = build_camera_name_map()
def get_camera_display_name(source):
    """يعيد اسم الكاميرا الواضح إذا كان موجودًا، أو اسم الملف للفيديو، أو المصدر نفسه."""
    src = str(source).strip()
    # إذا كان فيديو محلي
    if src.lower().endswith(('.mp4', '.avi', '.mov', '.mkv')):
        return os.path.basename(src)
    # إذا كان في القاموس
    return CAMERA_NAME_MAP.get(src, src)

class LoginDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setWindowTitle("تسجيل الدخول")
        self.setModal(True) 
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        form_layout = QGridLayout()
        form_layout.addWidget(QLabel("اسم المستخدم:"), 0, 0)
        self.username_edit = QLineEdit()
        form_layout.addWidget(self.username_edit, 0, 1)
        form_layout.addWidget(QLabel("كلمة المرور:"), 1, 0)
        self.password_edit = QLineEdit()
        self.password_edit.setEchoMode(QLineEdit.Password) 
        form_layout.addWidget(self.password_edit, 1, 1)
        layout.addLayout(form_layout)
        
        button_box = QHBoxLayout()
        self.login_button = QPushButton("دخول")
        self.login_button.clicked.connect(self.accept) 
        cancel_button = QPushButton("إلغاء")
        cancel_button.clicked.connect(self.reject) 
        button_box.addStretch(1)
        button_box.addWidget(self.login_button)
        button_box.addWidget(cancel_button)
        layout.addLayout(button_box)
        # تغيير حجم النافذة قليلاً
        self.setMinimumWidth(300)
    def getCredentials(self):
        """ الحصول على اسم المستخدم وكلمة المرور المدخلة """
        return self.username_edit.text().strip(), self.password_edit.text()

def authenticate_user(username, password):
    """
    التحقق من اسم المستخدم وكلمة المرور مقابل ملف users.json.
    ترجع دور المستخدم (role) إذا كانت المصادقة ناجحة، وإلا ترجع None.
    """
    if not BCRYPT_AVAILABLE:
        print("خطأ: مكتبة bcrypt غير متاحة للمصادقة.")
        return None # لا يمكن المصادقة بدون bcrypt
    if not os.path.exists(USERS_FILE):
        print(f"خطأ: ملف المستخدمين {USERS_FILE} غير موجود.")
        return None
    try:
        with open(USERS_FILE, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            users_data = json.load(f)
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, IOError) as e:
        print(f"خطأ في قراءة ملف المستخدمين: {e}")
        return None
    if not isinstance(users_data, list):
        print(f"خطأ: تنسيق ملف المستخدمين غير صحيح (يجب أن يكون قائمة).")
        return None
    for user in users_data:
        if user.get("username") == username:
            stored_hash = user.get("password_hash")
            if stored_hash:
                try:
                    # تحويل كلمة المرور المدخلة والـ hash المخزن إلى bytes
                    password_bytes = password.encode('utf-8')
                    stored_hash_bytes = stored_hash.encode('utf-8')
                    
                    if bcrypt.checkpw(password_bytes, stored_hash_bytes):
                        print(f"المصادقة ناجحة للمستخدم: {username}")
                        return user.get("role", "unknown") 
                    else:
                        print(f"كلمة مرور خاطئة للمستخدم: {username}")
                        return None 
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"خطأ أثناء التحقق من كلمة المرور لـ {username}: {e}")
                    return None 
            else:
                print(f"تحذير: لا يوجد hash لكلمة المرور للمستخدم: {username}")
                return None 
    print(f"المستخدم '{username}' غير موجود.")
    return None 

import threading
class ExcelLogger:
    def __init__(self, filename=LOG_FILE):
        self.filename = filename
        self._check_file()
        self._pending = []
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._stop_event = threading.Event()
        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._writer_loop, daemon=True)
        self._thread.start()
    def _check_file(self):
        if not PANDAS_AVAILABLE: return
        if not os.path.exists(self.filename):
            df = pd.DataFrame(columns=['Timestamp', 'Source', 'Direction', 'ObjectID', 'TotalCount', 'Width', 'Height'])
            try:
                df.to_excel(self.filename, index=False, engine='openpyxl')
                print(f"تم إنشاء ملف السجل: {self.filename}")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"خطأ في إنشاء ملف Excel: {e}")
    def log_crossing(self, source_name, direction, object_id, total_count, width=0, height=0):
        if not PANDAS_AVAILABLE:
            print("خطأ: مكتبة pandas غير متاحة للتسجيل.")
            return
        timestamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
        entry = {
            'Timestamp': timestamp,
             'Source': get_camera_display_name(source_name),  
            'Direction': direction,
            'ObjectID': object_id,
            'TotalCount': total_count,
            'Width': width,
            'Height': height
        }
        with self._lock:
            self._pending.append(entry)
    def _writer_loop(self):
        while not self._stop_event.is_set():
            self._flush_pending()
            self._stop_event.wait(2)  
    def _flush_pending(self):
        with self._lock:
            if not self._pending:
                return
            entries = self._pending[:]
            self._pending.clear()
        try:
            try:
                df = pd.read_excel(self.filename, engine='openpyxl')
            except FileNotFoundError:
                self._check_file()
                df = pd.DataFrame(columns=['Timestamp', 'Source', 'Direction', 'ObjectID', 'TotalCount', 'Width', 'Height'])
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"خطأ غير متوقع عند قراءة ملف Excel: {e}")
                df = pd.DataFrame(columns=['Timestamp', 'Source', 'Direction', 'ObjectID', 'TotalCount', 'Width', 'Height'])
            new_df = pd.DataFrame(entries)
            df = pd.concat([df, new_df], ignore_index=True)
            df.to_excel(self.filename, index=False, engine='openpyxl')
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"خطأ في الكتابة إلى ملف Excel (غير متزامن): {e}")

    def stop(self):
        self._stop_event.set()
        self._thread.join()
        self._flush_pending()
def get_gst_pipeline(source_url: str) -> str:
    return (
        f"rtspsrc location={source_url} latency= 0 ! "
        "rtph264depay ! h264parse ! avdec_h264 ! videoconvert ! appsink sync=false drop=0"
    )

class VideoProcessor(QThread):
    frame_processed = pyqtSignal(np.ndarray,int,int,int,int,set,set,set,set, np.ndarray) 
    object_crossed = pyqtSignal(str, str, int, int, int, int)  # source, direction, obj_id, count, width, height 
    processing_finished = pyqtSignal()
    error_signal = pyqtSignal(str)
    
    def __init__(self, video_source, config, source_name="Default",
                 display_line_points=None, line_direction=None,
                 display_region_points=None, 
                 display_region2_points=None, 
                 display_polygon_points=None,
                 display_size=None, original_display_size=None, reid_model=None,
                 **kwargs): # Accept extra arguments
        super().__init__()
        print(f"[DEBUG PROCESSOR INIT - RAW ARGS] display_size type: {type(display_size)}, value: {display_size}")
        self.video_source = video_source
        self.config = config
        # self.system_settings= settings
        self.source_name = source_name
        self.running = True
        self.cap = None
        # --- Open VideoCapture on initialization ---
        try:
            # --- تعديل: محاولة تحويل المصدر إلى رقم صحيح (لكاميرات الويب) ---
            # هذا يضمن أن مصادر مثل "0", "1" يتم التعامل معها كأرقام.
            capture_source = int(self.video_source)
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            capture_source = self.video_source

        if capture_source is None:
            print(f"[ERROR] Video source for {self.source_name} is None. Cannot initialize.")
            self.cap = None
        else:
            # --- تعديل: استخدام VideoStream لتحسين الاستقرار ---
            # سنقوم بإنشاء كائن VideoStream هنا ولكن لا نبدأ التشغيل بعد
            print(f"Initializing VideoStream for: {capture_source}")
            # self.v_stream = VideoStream(capture_source).start()
            # if self.v_stream.stream.isOpened():
            #     print(f"[DEBUG] Video stream for {capture_source} opened successfully via VideoStream.")
            # else:
            #     print(f"[ERROR] Failed to open video stream for {capture_source} via VideoStream.")
            #     self.v_stream = None
            # ----------------------------------------------------
            # --- تعديل: العودة إلى الطريقة القديمة مؤقتًا مع تحسينات ---
            self.cap = cv2.VideoCapture(capture_source)
            if not self.cap.isOpened():
                print(f"[ERROR] Initial VideoCapture open failed for {self.source_name}. Will retry in run().")
            else:
                print(f"[DEBUG] Initial VideoCapture open successful for {self.source_name}.")
        #self.model = yolo_model # نموذج YOLO
        self.tracked_objects = {}
        self.object_history = {}
        self.next_object_id = 1
        self.frame_count = 0
        self.frames_to_skip = config.get('frames_to_skip', 0)
        self.bg_subtractor = cv2.createBackgroundSubtractorMOG2(history=300, varThreshold=16, detectShadows=False) 
        self.display_size = display_size # الحجم الحالي للـ QLabel الذي يعرض هذا المعالج
        self.original_display_size = original_display_size if original_display_size else display_size # <-- تخزين الحجم الأصلي
        self.last_seen = {}      # {id: (frame_idx, (x, y))}
        self.id_map = {}         # {new_id: old_id}
        self.crossed_ids = set() # IDs التي عبرت الخط
        self.crossing_threshold = 0.05  
        self.cross_log = []      # لتسجيل تفاصيل العبور
        # --- إعدادات الخط ---
        self.initial_display_line_points = display_line_points
        self.initial_line_direction = line_direction
        self.line_points = None # (p1_scaled, p2_scaled) or None
        self.line_direction = self.initial_line_direction
        self.line_crossings = 0
        self.line_crossed_ids_this_session = set()
        self.last_line_crossing_time = 0 
        self.line_crossing_cooldown = 190 
        # --- إعدادات المنطقة ---
        self.initial_display_region_points = display_region_points
        self.scaled_region_rect = None 
        self.region_crossings = 0
        self.region_entered_ids_this_session = set() 
        self.last_region_entry_time = 0 
        self.region_entry_cooldown = 190 
        # --- إعدادات المنطقة 2 ---
        self.initial_display_region2_points = display_region2_points
        self.scaled_region2_rect = None # (x1, y1, x2, y2) or None
        self.region2_crossings = 0
        self.region2_entered_ids_this_session = set()
        self.last_region2_entry_time = 0
      #  self.region2_entry_cooldown = 1.5       
        self.region2_entry_cooldown = 190
        # --- إعدادات المضلع ---
        self.initial_display_polygon_points = display_polygon_points # قائمة من QPoint
        self.scaled_polygon_points = None # قائمة من tuple (x, y)
        self.polygon_crossings = 0
        self.polygon_entered_ids_this_session = set()
        self.last_polygon_entry_time = 0
        self.polygon_entry_cooldown = 180 # Cooldown in seconds
        self.polygon_entry_cooldown = config.get('polygon_entry_cooldown', 180) # Cooldown in seconds        # --- إعدادات العرض والمعالجة ---
        self.display_size = display_size
        self.target_width = config.get('target_width', 640)
        self.target_height = config.get('target_height', 384)
        self.first_frame_processed = False 
        self.is_polygon_occupied = False       # هل المنطقة مشغولة حالياً؟
        self.last_time_polygon_occupied = 0.0  # آخر وقت رأينا فيه ونش بالداخل
        self.ZOOM_CLEARANCE_BUFFER = 30.0      # (ثانية) فترة الأمان لتفريغ المنطقة

        # --- تعديل: حساب الإحداثيات المقاسة فورًا عند الإنشاء ---
        self.line_points = self._calculate_scaled_points()
        self.scaled_region_rect = self._calculate_scaled_region_rect()
        self.scaled_region2_rect = self._calculate_scaled_region2_rect()
        self.scaled_polygon_points = self._calculate_scaled_polygon()
        # ---------------------------------------------------------

        print(f"[DEBUG] Initializing VideoProcessor ({self.source_name}):")
        print(f"  - Line: points={self.initial_display_line_points}, direction={self.initial_line_direction}")
        print(f"  - Region: points={self.initial_display_region_points}")
        print(f"  - Display Size: {self.display_size}")
        print(f"  - Target Size: {self.target_width}x{self.target_height}")
        print(f"  - Frames to skip: {self.frames_to_skip}")
        print(f"  - Original Display Size (for scaling): {self.original_display_size}") 
        print(f"[DEBUG PROCESSOR INIT] Source '{self.source_name}' - Received Original Size: {self.original_display_size}, Received Current Size: {self.display_size}")
        self.device = 'cuda' if TORCH_AVAILABLE and torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu'
        print(f"[DEBUG Processor] Using device: {self.device}")
        self.tracker = None
        
        # --- State Machine & ID Stitching Variables ---
        self.object_states = {}  # {obj_id: {'state': 'outside'|'inside'|'counted', 'last_seen_inside': timestamp, 'first_seen_inside': timestamp}}
        self.tombstones = {}     # {obj_id: {'centroid': (x,y), 'last_seen': timestamp, 'bbox': [...]}}
        self.id_map = {}         # {new_id: old_id} - To map temporary new IDs to recovered old IDs
        
        # --- Constants ---
        self.STITCH_DIST_THRESHOLD = 150  # Pixels: Max distance to recover an ID
        self.STITCH_TIME_THRESHOLD = 10.0  # Seconds: Max time to recover an ID
        self.polygon_reset_threshold = 120.0 # Seconds: Increased to 60s to prevent premature reset
        self.MIN_TIME_INSIDE = 0.0        # Seconds: Minimum time inside before counting (optional, can be 0)

        tracker_type = self.config.get('tracker_type', "deepocsort")
        min_conf = self.config.get('min_confidence', 0.55)
        print(f"--- [VERIFICATION - PID: {os.getpid()}] Camera: {self.source_name} ---")

        print(f"    - Using min_confidence: {min_conf}")

                        if torch.cuda.is_available():

    def get_point_side(self, line_p1, line_p2, point):
           """ يحسب قيمة تحدد جانب النقطة بالنسبة للخط (p1 -> p2). إشارة القيمة تحدد الجانب."""
           try:
                x1, y1 = line_p1
                x2, y2 = line_p2
                px, py = point
                val = (x2 - x1) * (py - y1) - (y2 - y1) * (px - x1)
                return val
           except Exception as e:
                 print(f"[ERROR] get_point_side failed: {e}")
                 return 0 # التعامل مع الخطأ بإرجاع 0 (كأن النقطة على الخط)
    def _calculate_scaled_coordinates(self, display_points, display_size_when_drawn):
        """
        يحسب إحداثيات مقاسة لنقاط الخط أو المنطقة مع مراعاة KeepAspectRatio.
        display_points: tuple of QPoint (p1_display, p2_display)
        display_size: QSize of the QLabel
        """
        # --- تعديل: الاعتماد دائمًا على الحجم الأصلي المحفوظ للحساب ---
        display_size_when_drawn = self.original_display_size
        # -----------------------------------------------------------

        # طباعة تصحيحية للتحقق من القيم المستلمة
        # print(f"[DEBUG SCALE] {self.source_name} - display_points={display_points}, display_size_when_drawn={display_size_when_drawn}, target={self.target_width}x{self.target_height}")

        if not display_points or not display_size_when_drawn or not self.target_width or not self.target_height:
            print("[WARN] _calculate_scaled_coordinates: Missing input data.")
            return None
        if display_size_when_drawn.width() <= 0 or display_size_when_drawn.height() <= 0:
            print(f"[WARN] _calculate_scaled_coordinates: Invalid display_size: {display_size_when_drawn}")
            return None
        try:
            p1_display, p2_display = display_points
            display_w = display_size_when_drawn.width()
            display_h = display_size_when_drawn.height()
            proc_w = self.target_width
            proc_h = self.target_height
            img_ratio = proc_w / proc_h
            label_ratio = display_w / display_h
            actual_pixmap_w = 0
            actual_pixmap_h = 0
            if label_ratio > img_ratio:
                actual_pixmap_h = display_h
                actual_pixmap_w = actual_pixmap_h * img_ratio
            else:
                actual_pixmap_w = display_w
                actual_pixmap_h = actual_pixmap_w / img_ratio
            if actual_pixmap_w <= 0 or actual_pixmap_h <= 0:
                 print(f"[WARN] _calculate_scaled_coordinates: Invalid calculated pixmap dimensions: {actual_pixmap_w}x{actual_pixmap_h}")
                 return None
            # حساب الإزاحة (الأشرطة السوداء)
            offset_x = (display_w - actual_pixmap_w) / 2
            offset_y = (display_h - actual_pixmap_h) / 2
            # حساب عوامل القياس (يجب أن تكون متساوية)
            scale_x = proc_w / actual_pixmap_w
            scale_y = proc_h / actual_pixmap_h
            scaled_points_list = []
            for p_display in [p1_display, p2_display]:
                if not isinstance(p_display, QPoint): # تحقق إضافي
                    print(f"[WARN] _calculate_scaled_coordinates: Invalid display point type: {type(p_display)}")
                    return None
                # 1. تحويل إحداثيات العرض إلى إحداثيات نسبة للـ Pixmap الفعلي (إزالة الأشرطة السوداء)
                pixmap_x = p_display.x() - offset_x
                pixmap_y = p_display.y() - offset_y
               # 2. تحويل إحداثيات الـ Pixmap إلى إحداثيات المعالجة
                processing_x = pixmap_x * scale_x
                processing_y = pixmap_y * scale_y
                # 3. التأكد من أن الإحداثيات ضمن حدود إطار المعالجة
                final_x = max(0, min(proc_w - 1, int(round(processing_x)))) # تقريب لأقرب عدد صحيح
                final_y = max(0, min(proc_h - 1, int(round(processing_y))))
                scaled_points_list.append((final_x, final_y))
            return tuple(scaled_points_list) # إرجاع (p1_scaled, p2_scaled)
        except ZeroDivisionError:
            print("[ERROR] _calculate_scaled_coordinates: Division by zero encountered.")
            return None
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[ERROR] _calculate_scaled_coordinates failed: {type(e).__name__} - {e}")
            import traceback
            traceback.print_exc()
            return None
    def _calculate_scaled_polygon(self):
        """يحسب إحداثيات المضلع المقاسة."""
        if not self.initial_display_polygon_points or not self.original_display_size:
            return None
        
        scaled_points = []
        # نقوم بتقسيم القائمة إلى أزواج من النقاط لحساب كل ضلع
        for i in range(len(self.initial_display_polygon_points)):
            p1_display = self.initial_display_polygon_points[i]
            # نستخدم النقطة نفسها كنقطة ثانية لأن الدالة تتوقع زوجًا
            scaled_pair = self._calculate_scaled_coordinates((p1_display, p1_display), self.original_display_size)
            if scaled_pair:
                scaled_points.append(scaled_pair[0]) # نأخذ النقطة الأولى فقط من الزوج المحسوب
        return scaled_points if len(scaled_points) > 2 else None
    def _calculate_scaled_points(self):
        """يحسب إحداثيات الخط المقاسة."""
        return self._calculate_scaled_coordinates(self.initial_display_line_points, self.original_display_size)
    def _calculate_scaled_region_rect(self):
        """Calculates scaled coordinates for the region rect."""
        scaled_points = self._calculate_scaled_coordinates(self.initial_display_region_points, self.original_display_size)
        if not scaled_points: return None
        p1_scaled, p2_scaled = scaled_points
        x1 = min(p1_scaled[0], p2_scaled[0])
        y1 = min(p1_scaled[1], p2_scaled[1])
        x2 = max(p1_scaled[0], p2_scaled[0])
        y2 = max(p1_scaled[1], p2_scaled[1])
        if x1 >= x2 or y1 >= y2: return None
        return (x1, y1, x2, y2)
    def _calculate_scaled_region2_rect(self):
        """Calculates scaled coordinates for the region 2 rect."""
        scaled_points = self._calculate_scaled_coordinates(self.initial_display_region2_points, self.original_display_size)
        if not scaled_points: return None
        p1_scaled, p2_scaled = scaled_points
        x1 = min(p1_scaled[0], p2_scaled[0])
        y1 = min(p1_scaled[1], p2_scaled[1])
        x2 = max(p1_scaled[0], p2_scaled[0])
        y2 = max(p1_scaled[1], p2_scaled[1])
        if x1 >= x2 or y1 >= y2: 
            print(f"[DEBUG] REGION2: invalid rect ({x1},{y1},{x2},{y2}) for {self.source_name}")
            return None
        print(f"[DEBUG] Processor ({self.source_name}) calculated scaled REGION 2 rect: {(x1, y1, x2, y2)}")
        return (x1, y1, x2, y2)
    def _draw_direction_arrow_cv(self, frame, p1, p2):
        """يرسم سهم الاتجاه على الإطار باستخدام OpenCV."""
        if not self.line_direction or not p1 or not p2: return
        try:
            mid_x = (p1[0] + p2[0]) // 2
            mid_y = (p1[1] + p2[1]) // 2
            arrow_len = 10
            arrow_width = 5 # نصف عرض قاعدة السهم
            arrow_dx, arrow_dy = 0, 0
            if self.line_direction == 'ltr': arrow_dx = arrow_len
            elif self.line_direction == 'rtl': arrow_dx = -arrow_len
            elif self.line_direction == 'ttb': arrow_dy = arrow_len
            elif self.line_direction == 'btt': arrow_dy = -arrow_len
            else: return
            tip_x, tip_y = mid_x + arrow_dx, mid_y + arrow_dy
            perp_arrow_x = -arrow_dy
            perp_arrow_y = arrow_dx
            norm = np.sqrt(perp_arrow_x**2 + perp_arrow_y**2)
            if norm > 1e-6:
               perp_arrow_x = (perp_arrow_x / norm) * arrow_width
               perp_arrow_y = (perp_arrow_y / norm) * arrow_width
            else:
               perp_arrow_x = arrow_width if arrow_dy != 0 else 0
               perp_arrow_y = arrow_width if arrow_dx != 0 else 0
            base1_x, base1_y = int(mid_x + perp_arrow_x), int(mid_y + perp_arrow_y)
            base2_x, base2_y = int(mid_x - perp_arrow_x), int(mid_y - perp_arrow_y)
        # إنشاء مصفوفة نقاط المثلث لـ fillPoly
            pts = np.array([[tip_x, tip_y], [base1_x, base1_y], [base2_x, base2_y]], np.int32)
            pts = pts.reshape((-1, 1, 2))
        # الحصول على لون الخط BGR
            line_color_bgr = (DEFAULT_LINE_COLOR.blue(), DEFAULT_LINE_COLOR.green(), DEFAULT_LINE_COLOR.red())
        # رسم المثلث المعبأ
            cv2.fillPoly(frame, [pts], line_color_bgr)
        except Exception as e:
           print(f"خطأ في رسم سهم الاتجاه (CV): {e}")
    # داخل فئة VideoProcessor في ملف main.py
    def run(self):
            if not self.model and YOLO_AVAILABLE: # تحقق إذا تم تمرير النموذج
                 self.error_signal.emit("لم يتم توفير نموذج YOLO للمعالج.")
                 self.running = False
                 self.processing_finished.emit()
                 return
            if not self.running: # تحقق مبكر من حالة التشغيل
                self.processing_finished.emit()
                return
            
            self.cap = None
            try:
                source_str = str(self.video_source)
                # الحالة 1: بث RTSP -> استخدم GStreamer
                if source_str.lower().startswith("rtsp://"):
                    print(f"محاولة فتح بث RTSP باستخدام GStreamer لـ {self.source_name}...")
                    pipeline = get_gst_pipeline(source_str) # استخدام الدالة المساعدة الجديدة
                    self.cap = cv2.VideoCapture(pipeline, cv2.CAP_GSTREAMER)
                else:
                    # الحالة 2: كاميرا ويب (رقم) أو ملف فيديو محلي
                    try:
                        # محاولة تحويله إلى رقم (لكاميرا الويب)
                        capture_source = int(source_str)
                        print(f"فتح كاميرا الويب المحلية: {capture_source} لـ {self.source_name} باستخدام الواجهة الافتراضية.")
                        # استخدم الواجهة الافتراضية (CAP_ANY) فهي أكثر استقرارًا لكاميرات الويب
                        self.cap = cv2.VideoCapture(capture_source, cv2.CAP_ANY)
                    except ValueError:
                        # إذا فشل التحويل، فهو مسار ملف
                        capture_source = source_str
                        print(f"فتح ملف فيديو محلي: {capture_source} لـ {self.source_name} باستخدام الواجهة الافتراضية.")
                        # استخدم الواجهة الافتراضية (CAP_ANY) للملفات أيضًا
                        self.cap = cv2.VideoCapture(capture_source, cv2.CAP_ANY)
            except Exception as e_open:
                print(f"خطأ غير متوقع أثناء محاولة فتح مصدر الفيديو: {e_open}")

           # self.cap = cv2.VideoCapture(capture_source, cv2.CAP_FFMPEG)
            # محاولة تعيين مهلة القراءة (قد لا تكون مدعومة من جميع الواجهات الخلفية)
            read_timeout_supported = self.cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_READ_TIMEOUT_MSEC, 5000) # 5 ثوانٍ
            if read_timeout_supported:
                print(f"[DEBUG] {self.source_name}: تم محاولة تعيين CAP_PROP_READ_TIMEOUT_MSEC إلى 5000ms.")
            else:
               print(f"[WARN] {self.source_name}: CAP_PROP_READ_TIMEOUT_MSEC غير مدعوم من الواجهة الخلفية.")
            if not self.cap.isOpened():
                error_msg = f"فشل فتح مصدر الفيديو: {self.video_source} لـ {self.source_name}"
                print(error_msg)
                self.error_signal.emit(error_msg)
                self.running = False
                self.processing_finished.emit()
                return
            else:
                print(f"مصدر الفيديو مفتوح: {self.video_source} لـ {self.source_name}")
            try:
                self.tracked_objects = {}
                self.object_history = {}
                self.line_crossings = 0
                self.line_crossed_ids_this_session = set()
                self.region_crossings = 0
                self.region_entered_ids_this_session = set()
                self.region2_crossings = 0
                self.region2_entered_ids_this_session = set()
                self.polygon_crossings = 0
                self.polygon_entered_ids_this_session = set()
                self.frame_count = 0
                self.first_frame_processed = False
                if self.tracker and hasattr(self.tracker, 'reset'):
                    try:
                        self.tracker.reset()
                        print(f"تم إعادة تعيين حالة المتتبع لـ {self.source_name}")
                    except Exception as e_reset:
                        print(f"خطأ أثناء محاولة إعادة تعيين المتتبع لـ {self.source_name}: {e_reset}")
                read_error_count = 0
                max_read_errors_total = 30 # إجمالي عدد مرات فشل القراءة المسموح به
                consecutive_read_failures = 0
                max_consecutive_read_failures = 10 # عدد مرات فشل القراءة المتتالية المسموح به
                while self.running:
                    if not self.running: # تحقق إضافي في بداية كل دورة
                        print(f"[DEBUG] {self.source_name}: الخروج من الحلقة في البداية بسبب طلب الإيقاف.")
                        break
                    grabbed = False
                    frame = None
                    ret = False
                    try:
                        grabbed = self.cap.grab()
                        #print(f"[DEBUG] {self.source_name}: cap.grab() returned {grabbed}")
                        if grabbed:
                            consecutive_read_failures = 0 # إعادة تعيين عند النجاح
                            if not self.running: break # تحقق بعد grab
                            ret, frame = self.cap.retrieve()
                           # print(f"[DEBUG] {self.source_name}: cap.retrieve() returned {ret}")
                            if not ret:
                                print(f"[WARN] {self.source_name}: cap.retrieve() فشل بعد grab ناجح.")
                        else:
                            consecutive_read_failures += 1
                            print(f"[DEBUG] {self.source_name}: cap.grab() فشل. فشل متتالي: {consecutive_read_failures}")
                            ret = False # Ensure ret is False so the next block handles loop exit
                    except cv2.error as cv_err:
                         print(f"[ERROR] {self.source_name}: خطأ OpenCV أثناء grab/retrieve: {cv_err}")
                         ret = False # اعتبرها فشلًا
                         consecutive_read_failures += 1
                    except Exception as e_grab:
                        print(f"[ERROR] {self.source_name}: خطأ عام أثناء grab/retrieve: {e_grab}")
                        ret = False # اعتبرها فشلًا
                        consecutive_read_failures += 1
                    if not ret: # إذا فشل grab أو retrieve
                        if not grabbed: # If grab failed, this means EOF or critical error.
                            print(f"[INFO] {self.source_name}: cap.grab() فشل. افتراض نهاية البث أو خطأ لا يمكن استرداده.")
                            # التحقق مما إذا كان الفشل بسبب تجاوز حدود الفشل المتتالي لـ grab
                            if consecutive_read_failures >= max_consecutive_read_failures:
                                error_msg_detail = f"(تجاوز حد الفشل المتتالي للقراءة: {max_consecutive_read_failures})"
                                final_error_msg = f"فشل قراءة/استرداد البث بشكل متكرر من: {self.source_name}. {error_msg_detail}"
                                print(final_error_msg)
                                self.error_signal.emit(final_error_msg)
                            self.running = False # Ensure the loop condition is false for a clean exit
                            break # Exit the loop directly
                        read_error_count += 1
                        print(f"خطأ قراءة/استرداد الإطار ({self.source_name}) - المحاولة {read_error_count}/{max_read_errors_total}, فشل متتالي: {consecutive_read_failures}/{max_consecutive_read_failures}")

                        if consecutive_read_failures >= max_consecutive_read_failures or read_error_count >= max_read_errors_total:
                           error_msg_detail = ""
                           if consecutive_read_failures >= max_consecutive_read_failures:
                               error_msg_detail = f"(تجاوز حد الفشل المتتالي للقراءة: {max_consecutive_read_failures})"
                           else:
                               error_msg_detail = f"(تجاوز إجمالي أخطاء القراءة: {max_read_errors_total})"
                           final_error_msg = f"فشل قراءة/استرداد البث بشكل متكرر من: {self.source_name}. {error_msg_detail}"
                           print(final_error_msg)
                           self.error_signal.emit(final_error_msg)
                           self.running = False
                           break # الخروج من حلقة while
                        wait_interval_ms = 200
                        num_sleeps = 5 # تقسيم الانتظار للتحقق من running
                        for _ in range(num_sleeps):
                            if not self.running: break
                            self.msleep(wait_interval_ms // num_sleeps)
                        if not self.running:
                            print(f"[DEBUG] {self.source_name}: الخروج من الحلقة بعد الانتظار بسبب طلب الإيقاف.")
                            break
                        continue # انتقل للمحاولة التالية
                    read_error_count = 0 # إعادة تعيين عداد الأخطاء الكلي عند النجاح
                    if not self.first_frame_processed:
                        if not self.running: break
                        self.line_points = self._calculate_scaled_points()
                        print(f"[DEBUG] {self.source_name} - SCALED line_points: {self.line_points}")
                        self.scaled_region_rect = self._calculate_scaled_region_rect()
                        print(f"[DEBUG] {self.source_name} - SCALED region_rect: {self.scaled_region_rect}")
                        self.scaled_region2_rect = self._calculate_scaled_region2_rect()
                        print(f"[DEBUG] {self.source_name} - SCALED region2_rect: {self.scaled_region2_rect}")
                        self.scaled_polygon_points = self._calculate_scaled_polygon()
                        print(f"[DEBUG] {self.source_name} - SCALED polygon_points: {self.scaled_polygon_points}")

                        self.first_frame_processed = True
                    self.frame_count += 1
                    if self.frames_to_skip > 0 and self.frame_count % (self.frames_to_skip + 1) != 0:
                        continue
                    if not self.running: break                 
                    try:
                        resized_frame = cv2.resize(frame, (self.target_width, self.target_height), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
                    except cv2.error as e_resize:
                        print(f"خطأ تغيير حجم الإطار ({self.source_name}): {e_resize}"); continue
                    processed_frame = resized_frame

                    # --- YOLO Detection and TTA (Refactored) ---
                    detections_raw = self.perform_detection(processed_frame)

                    if not self.running: break

                    # --- Size Filtering (Refactored) ---
                    filtered_detections_array = self.filter_detections_by_size(detections_raw)

                    # --- Tracker Update (Refactored) ---
                    tracked_bboxes, current_centroids = self.update_tracker(filtered_detections_array, resized_frame)

                    if not self.running: break

                    # --- Crossing Logic (Refactored) ---
                    self.update_crossing_logic(tracked_bboxes, current_centroids, resized_frame)

                    if not self.running: break
                    # --- تعديل: لا تقم بالرسم هنا، أرسل البيانات الخام ---
                    # display_frame = self.draw_on_frame(processed_frame, tracked_bboxes)
                    if not self.running: break
                    try:
                        self.frame_processed.emit( # إرسال الإطار الأصلي مع بيانات التتبع
                            cv2.cvtColor(processed_frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB),
                            self.line_crossings, self.region_crossings, self.region2_crossings,
                            #self.line_crossed_ids_this_session.copy(),
                            self.polygon_crossings, 
                            self.polygon_entered_ids_this_session.copy(),
                            self.line_crossed_ids_this_session.copy(),
                            self.region_entered_ids_this_session.copy(),
                            self.region2_entered_ids_this_session.copy()
                            , tracked_bboxes # <-- إضافة: إرسال بيانات التتبع
                        )
                    except cv2.error as e_cvt:
                        print(f"خطأ تحويل ألوان الإطار ({self.source_name}): {e_cvt}"); continue
                # --- نهاية حلقة while self.running ---
                print(f"[DEBUG] {self.source_name}: خرج من حلقة المعالجة الرئيسية. self.running حاليًا: {self.running}")
            except Exception as loop_exception:
                 print(f"خطأ فادح داخل حلقة المعالجة الرئيسية ({self.source_name}): {loop_exception}")
                 import traceback
                 traceback.print_exc()
                 self.error_signal.emit(f"خطأ فادح في المعالج ({self.source_name}): {loop_exception}")
                 # لا تقم بتعيين self.running = False هنا، دع finally يتعامل معها
            finally:
                print(f"تنظيف الموارد للمعالج ({self.source_name}) في finally بلوك...")
                if self.cap:
                    try:
                        if self.cap.isOpened():
                            print(f"محاولة تحرير VideoCapture لـ {self.source_name}...")
                            self.cap.release()
                            print(f"تم تحرير VideoCapture لـ {self.source_name}.")
                    except cv2.error as release_cv_err:
                          print(f"[ERROR] {self.source_name}: خطأ OpenCV أثناء تحرير VideoCapture: {release_cv_err}")
                    except Exception as e_release_generic:
                          print(f"[ERROR] {self.source_name}: خطأ عام أثناء تحرير VideoCapture: {e_release_generic}")
                    finally:
                        self.cap = None # تأكد من تعيينه إلى None
                # تأكد من أن running مضبوط على False هنا لضمان أن isRunning() سترجع False
                # هذا مهم بشكل خاص إذا خرجنا من الحلقة بسبب خطأ وليس بسبب self.running = False
                self.running = False
                print(f"[DEBUG] {self.source_name}: إرسال إشارة processing_finished من finally. self.running الآن: {self.running}")
                self.processing_finished.emit()
                print(f"تم إيقاف حلقة المعالجة وتنظيف الموارد بشكل كامل ({self.source_name}).")

    def perform_detection(self, frame):
        """Performs YOLO detection on a frame, including TTA if enabled."""
        all_tta_detections_list = []
        # 1. Original Detection
        try:
            results_original = self.model(frame, conf=self.config.get('min_confidence', 0.55), iou=self.config.get('max_iou', 0.50), imgsz=self.config.get('target_width', 640), classes=self.config.get('classes', [0]), agnostic_nms=True, verbose=False)
            if results_original and results_original[0].boxes is not None and len(results_original[0].boxes) > 0:
                all_tta_detections_list.append(results_original[0].boxes.data.cpu().numpy())
        except Exception as e_yolo_orig:            print(f"YOLO Error (Original) ({self.source_name}): {e_yolo_orig}")

        # 2. Flipped Detection (TTA)
        if self.config.get('apply_tta_flip', False):
            try:
                flipped_frame = cv2.flip(frame, 1)
                results_flipped = self.model(flipped_frame, conf=self.config.get('min_confidence', 0.55), iou=self.config.get('max_iou', 0.50), imgsz=self.config.get('target_width', 640), classes=self.config.get('classes', [0]), agnostic_nms=True, verbose=False)
                if results_flipped and results_flipped[0].boxes is not None and len(results_flipped[0].boxes) > 0:
                    detections_flipped_raw = results_flipped[0].boxes.data.cpu().numpy()
                    de_augmented_flipped_detections = detections_flipped_raw.copy()
                    frame_width = frame.shape[1]
                    de_augmented_flipped_detections[:, 0] = frame_width - detections_flipped_raw[:, 2]
                    de_augmented_flipped_detections[:, 2] = frame_width - detections_flipped_raw[:, 0]
                    all_tta_detections_list.append(de_augmented_flipped_detections)
            except Exception as e_yolo_flipped:
                print(f"YOLO Error (Flipped TTA) ({self.source_name}): {e_yolo_flipped}")

        # 3. Combine and Final NMS
        if not all_tta_detections_list:
            return np.empty((0, 6))
        
        combined_detections = np.vstack(all_tta_detections_list)
        if combined_detections.shape[0] == 0:
            return np.empty((0, 6))

        boxes_for_nms = combined_detections[:, :4]
        scores_for_nms = combined_detections[:, 4]
        final_nms_iou_threshold = self.config.get('final_nms_iou_threshold', 0.5)
        indices = cv2.dnn.NMSBoxes(boxes_for_nms.tolist(), scores_for_nms.tolist(), self.config.get('min_confidence', 0.55), final_nms_iou_threshold)

        if len(indices) > 0:
            return combined_detections[indices.flatten()]
        else:
            return np.empty((0, 6))

    def filter_detections_by_size(self, detections_raw):
        """Filters detected objects based on their bounding box size."""
        
        filtered_detections_array = np.empty((0, 6))
        
        if detections_raw.shape[0] > 0:
            min_w1 = self.config.get('min_box_width_type1', 250)
            max_w1 = self.config.get('max_box_width_type1', 600)
            min_h1 = self.config.get('min_box_height_type1', 320)
            max_h1 = self.config.get('max_box_height_type1', 500)

            min_w1 = max(1, min_w1); max_w1 = max(min_w1, max_w1)
            min_h1 = max(1, min_h1); max_h1 = max(min_h1, max_h1)
            classes = detections_raw[:, 5]
            if detections_raw.shape[0] > 0:
                widths = detections_raw[:, 2] - detections_raw[:, 0]
                heights = detections_raw[:, 3] - detections_raw[:, 1]
            filter_type1 = (classes == 0) & \
            ( widths >= min_w1) & (widths <= max_w1) & \
                        (heights >= min_h1) & (heights <= max_h1)
            combined_size_filter = filter_type1 
            final_filter = combined_size_filter
            filtered_detections_array = detections_raw[final_filter]
        return filtered_detections_array

    def recover_lost_id(self, new_id, new_centroid):
        """
        Attempts to recover a lost ID for a new object based on proximity and time.
        Returns the old_id if a match is found, otherwise None.
        """
        current_time = time.time()
        best_match_id = None
        min_dist = self.STITCH_DIST_THRESHOLD

        ids_to_remove = []

        for old_id, data in self.tombstones.items():
            last_seen_time = data['last_seen']
            
            # 1. Check Time Threshold
            if (current_time - last_seen_time) > self.STITCH_TIME_THRESHOLD:
                ids_to_remove.append(old_id)
                continue

            # 2. Check Distance Threshold
            old_centroid = data['centroid']
            dist = math.hypot(new_centroid[0] - old_centroid[0], new_centroid[1] - old_centroid[1])
            
            if dist < min_dist:
                min_dist = dist
                best_match_id = old_id

        # Clean up expired tombstones
        for old_id in ids_to_remove:
            del self.tombstones[old_id]

        if best_match_id:
            print(f"[ID STITCHING] Recovered ID {best_match_id} (Distance: {min_dist:.1f}px)")
            # Remove from tombstones as it is now active again
            del self.tombstones[best_match_id]
            return best_match_id
        
        return None

    def cleanup_tombstones(self):
        """Periodically clean up old tombstones and object states."""
        current_time = time.time()
        
        # 1. Clean up Tombstones (Short term: STITCH_TIME_THRESHOLD)
        ids_to_remove_tombstones = []
        for oid, data in self.tombstones.items():
            if (current_time - data['last_seen']) > self.STITCH_TIME_THRESHOLD:
                ids_to_remove_tombstones.append(oid)
        
        for oid in ids_to_remove_tombstones:
            del self.tombstones[oid]

        # 2. Clean up Object States (Long term: polygon_reset_threshold)
        # We keep states longer to prevent double counting if object returns
        ids_to_remove_states = []
        for oid, state_data in self.object_states.items():
            last_seen = state_data.get("last_seen_timestamp", 0)
            # If state is very old (older than reset threshold + buffer), remove it
            # We use polygon_reset_threshold (60s) as a safe upper bound
            if (current_time - last_seen) > (self.polygon_reset_threshold + 10.0):
                ids_to_remove_states.append(oid)
        
        for oid in ids_to_remove_states:
            del self.object_states[oid]

    def update_tracker(self, filtered_detections_array, resized_frame):
        """Updates the tracker with new detections and returns tracked bounding boxes and centroids."""
        tracked_bboxes = np.empty((0, 8))
        current_centroids = {}
        
        # 1. Get Tracker Outputs
        if self.tracker and filtered_detections_array.shape[0] > 0:
            try:
                outputs = self.tracker.update(filtered_detections_array, resized_frame)
                if outputs.shape[0] > 0:
                    tracked_bboxes = outputs
            except Exception as e_tracker:
                print(f"Error updating tracker ({self.source_name}): {e_tracker}")
                import traceback
                traceback.print_exc()
                tracked_bboxes = np.empty((0, 8))
        elif self.tracker:
            try:
                outputs = self.tracker.update(np.empty((0, 6)), resized_frame)
                if outputs.shape[0] > 0:
                    tracked_bboxes = outputs
            except Exception as e_tracker_empty:
                print(f"Error updating tracker (no detections) ({self.source_name}): {e_tracker_empty}")
                tracked_bboxes = np.empty((0, 8))

        # --- ID Stitching & Tombstone Logic ---
        current_active_ids = set()
        new_tracked_bboxes = []

        if tracked_bboxes.shape[0] > 0:
            for trk in tracked_bboxes:
                if len(trk) >= 5:
                    x1, y1, x2, y2, original_id = map(int, trk[:5])
                    cx, cy = (x1 + x2) // 2, (y1 + y2) // 2
                    
                    use_id = original_id

                    # 1. If mapped, use canonical ID
                    if use_id in self.id_map:
                        use_id = self.id_map[use_id]

                    # 2. If not in history -> try stitching
                    elif use_id not in self.object_history:
                        stitched = self.recover_lost_id(use_id, (cx, cy))
                        if stitched is not None:
                            self.id_map[use_id] = stitched
                            use_id = stitched

                    # 3. Use use_id for everything
                    trk[4] = use_id
                    
                    current_centroids[use_id] = (cx, cy)
                    current_active_ids.add(use_id)
                    new_tracked_bboxes.append(trk)
            
            tracked_bboxes = np.array(new_tracked_bboxes)

        # 2. Detect Lost IDs -> Create Tombstones
        if not hasattr(self, 'active_ids_last_frame'):
            self.active_ids_last_frame = set()
            
        lost_ids = self.active_ids_last_frame - current_active_ids
        current_time = time.time()
        
        for lost_id in lost_ids:
            # If we have history for this lost ID, create a tombstone
            if lost_id in self.object_history and self.object_history[lost_id]:
                last_data = self.object_history[lost_id][-1]
                last_pos = last_data['pos']
                # Store bbox if available, otherwise None
                last_bbox = last_data.get('bbox', None) 
                
                self.tombstones[lost_id] = {
                    'centroid': last_pos,
                    'bbox': last_bbox,
                    'last_seen': current_time
                }

        self.active_ids_last_frame = current_active_ids
        self.cleanup_tombstones()
        
        return tracked_bboxes, current_centroids

    def update_crossing_logic(self, tracked_bboxes, current_centroids,resized_frame):
        """Updates crossing counts and object history based on tracked bounding boxes."""
        active_ids_in_frame = set()
        # Store current bbox for each object ID (for width/height logging)
        self.current_object_bboxes = {}
        if tracked_bboxes.shape[0] > 0:
            for trk in tracked_bboxes:
                if len(trk) >= 5:
                    # NOTE: trk[4] is already use_id from update_tracker
                    use_id = int(trk[4])
                    active_ids_in_frame.add(use_id)
                    centroid = current_centroids.get(use_id)
                    if centroid:
                        x1, y1, x2, y2 = map(int, trk[:4])
                        # Store bbox dimensions for this object
                        self.current_object_bboxes[use_id] = {'width': x2 - x1, 'height': y2 - y1}
                        bottom_center  = ((x1 + x2) // 2, y2)
                        current_point_for_line = bottom_center
                        current_point_for_region = centroid
                        history = self.object_history.get(use_id, [])
                        prev_point_line = None
                        prev_point_region = None
                        if history:
                            prev_data = history[-1]
                            prev_point_line = prev_data.get('line_pos')
                            prev_point_region = prev_data.get('pos')
                        frame_with_drawings = self.draw_on_frame(resized_frame, tracked_bboxes)

                        if prev_point_line and prev_point_region:
                             self.check_line_crossing(use_id, prev_point_line, current_point_for_line,frame_with_drawings)
                             self.check_region_entry(use_id, prev_point_region, current_point_for_region,frame_with_drawings)
                             self.check_region2_entry(use_id, prev_point_region, current_point_for_region,frame_with_drawings)
                            # self.check_polygon_entry(use_id, prev_point_region, current_point_for_region,frame_with_drawings)
                        elif prev_point_region:
                             self.check_region_entry(use_id, prev_point_region, current_point_for_region,frame_with_drawings)
                             self.check_region2_entry(use_id, prev_point_region, current_point_for_region,frame_with_drawings)
                            # self.check_polygon_entry(use_id, prev_point_region, current_point_for_region,frame_with_drawings)
                        else:
                            self.check_region_entry(use_id, None, current_point_for_region, frame_with_drawings)
                            self.check_region2_entry(use_id, None, current_point_for_region, frame_with_drawings)
                        self.check_polygon_entry(use_id, None, current_point_for_region, frame_with_drawings)
                        current_data_to_store = {
                            'pos': current_point_for_region,
                            'line_pos': current_point_for_line,
                            'bbox': [x1, y1, x2, y2], # Store bbox for tombstone creation
                            'frame': self.frame_count
                        }
                        history.append(current_data_to_store)
                        self.object_history[use_id] = history
        
        # Cleanup history for lost IDs (Tombstones are created in update_tracker BEFORE this)
        ids_to_remove_from_history = set(self.object_history.keys()) - active_ids_in_frame
        for lost_id in ids_to_remove_from_history:
             if lost_id in self.object_history: del self.object_history[lost_id]
             self.line_crossed_ids_this_session.discard(lost_id)
             self.region_entered_ids_this_session.discard(lost_id)
             self.region2_entered_ids_this_session.discard(lost_id)
             if hasattr(self,'polygon_counted_ids'):
                 self.polygon_counted_ids.discard(lost_id)
             if hasattr(self,'region2_counted_ids'):
                 self.region2_counted_ids.discard(lost_id)    
        # ==============================================================================
        # منطق التصفير للمضلع (Polygon Reset Logic)
        # يتم فحصه في كل إطار بغض النظر عن الكائنات الموجودة
        # ==============================================================================
        current_time = time.time()
        
        # Debug: Log how many objects were detected this frame
        num_objects_in_frame = len(active_ids_in_frame)
        
        # إذا كانت المنطقة "مشغولة" (بناءً على آخر كائن تم رصده في check_polygon_entry)
        if self.is_polygon_occupied:
            # نحسب الزمن المنقضي منذ آخر مرة رأينا فيها أي ونش بالداخل
            time_since_last_seen = current_time - self.last_time_polygon_occupied
            
            # Debug: Show status every 5 seconds when zone is occupied
            # if int(time_since_last_seen) % 5 == 0 and int(time_since_last_seen) > 0:
            #     print(f"[DEBUG - {self.source_name}] Zone occupied. Time since last detection inside: {time_since_last_seen:.1f}s. "
            #           f"Objects in frame: {num_objects_in_frame}. Buffer: {self.ZOOM_CLEARANCE_BUFFER}s")
            
            # إذا مر وقت الأمان (ZOOM_CLEARANCE_BUFFER) دون رؤية أي كائن بالداخل
            if time_since_last_seen > self.ZOOM_CLEARANCE_BUFFER:
                self.is_polygon_occupied = False # تحرير المنطقة
                # Get count of globally tracked IDs for this camera
                ids_counted = len(getattr(self, 'globally_counted_polygon_ids', set()))
                print(f"[RESET - {self.source_name}] Polygon Zone Cleared after {time_since_last_seen:.1f}s. "
                      f"Total IDs counted this session: {ids_counted}. Total crossings: {self.polygon_crossings}. "
                      f"Ready for next winch.")

    def draw_on_frame(self, frame, tracked_bboxes):
        """يرسم كل العناصر (المربعات، الخطوط، المناطق) على الإطار."""
        """Draws bounding boxes, lines, and regions on the frame."""
        display_frame = frame.copy()
        if self.scaled_region_rect:
              rx1_s, ry1_s, rx2_s, ry2_s = self.scaled_region_rect
              cv2.rectangle(display_frame, (rx1_s, ry1_s), (rx2_s, ry2_s), (0, 255, 255), 1)
        if self.scaled_region2_rect:
            rx1_s2, ry1_s2, rx2_s2, ry2_s2 = self.scaled_region2_rect
            cv2.rectangle(display_frame, (rx1_s2, ry1_s2), (rx2_s2, ry2_s2), (255, 0, 255), 1)
        if self.scaled_polygon_points:
            pts = np.array(self.scaled_polygon_points, dtype=np.int32)
            pts = pts.reshape((-1, 1, 2))
            cv2.polylines(display_frame, [pts], isClosed=True, color=(0, 255, 0), thickness=1)
        if tracked_bboxes.shape[0] > 0:
            for trk in tracked_bboxes:
                if len(trk) >= 5:
                    x1, y1, x2, y2, track_id = map(int, trk[:5])
                    # Get confidence score if available (index 5)
                    conf = trk[5] if len(trk) > 5 else 0.0
                    color = (0, 255, 0)
                    if track_id in self.line_crossed_ids_this_session:
                        color = (255, 0, 0)
                    elif track_id in self.region_entered_ids_this_session:
                        color = (0, 0, 255)
                    elif hasattr(self, 'region2_counted_ids') and track_id in self.region2_counted_ids:
                        color = (255, 0, 255)
                    elif hasattr(self, 'polygon_counted_ids') and track_id in self.polygon_counted_ids:
                        color = (0, 255, 0) # أخضر للمضلع
                    cv2.rectangle(display_frame, (x1, y1), (x2, y2), color, 2)
                    # Display ID and confidence percentage
                    cv2.putText(display_frame, f"ID:{track_id} {conf*100:.0f}%", (x1, y1 - 10), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.5, color, 2)
        if self.line_points:
            p1_s, p2_s = self.line_points
            line_color_bgr_cv = (DEFAULT_LINE_COLOR.blue(), DEFAULT_LINE_COLOR.green(), DEFAULT_LINE_COLOR.red())
            cv2.line(display_frame, p1_s, p2_s, line_color_bgr_cv, 2)
            self._draw_direction_arrow_cv(display_frame, p1_s, p2_s)
        cv2.putText(display_frame, f"Line: {self.line_crossings}", (10, 30), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (255, 0, 0), 2)
        cv2.putText(display_frame, f"Region1: {self.region_crossings}", (10, 60), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 0, 255), 2)
        cv2.putText(display_frame, f"Region2: {self.region2_crossings}", (10, 90), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (255, 0, 255), 2)
        cv2.putText(display_frame, f"Polygon: {self.polygon_crossings}", (10, 120), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 255, 0), 2)
        return display_frame

    # --- تعديل: check_line_crossing لاستخدام العداد والـ set الخاص بالخط ---
    def check_line_crossing(self, obj_id, p_prev, p_curr,frame_to_save):
        """
        التحقق مما إذا كان الكائن obj_id قد عبر الخط المحدد (line_points) في الاتجاه المطلوب (line_direction).
        العد يتم مرة واحدة فقط لكل كائن في الجلسة.
        """
        if not self.line_points or not self.line_direction:
            return
        l1, l2 = self.line_points
        # --- التحقق من التقاطع الهندسي أولاً ---
        if not self.segment_intersection(p_prev, p_curr, l1, l2):
            return  # لا تقم بأي شيء إذا لم يكن هناك تقاطع هندسي
        try:
            # --- حساب متجه الخط ومتجه الحركة ---
            line_vec_x = l2[0] - l1[0]
            line_vec_y = l2[1] - l1[1]
            move_vec_x = p_curr[0] - p_prev[0]
            move_vec_y = p_curr[1] - p_prev[1]
            # تجنب القسمة على صفر أو متجهات صفرية
            line_length_sq = line_vec_x ** 2 + line_vec_y ** 2
            move_length_sq = move_vec_x ** 2 + move_vec_y ** 2
            if line_length_sq < 1e-9 or move_length_sq < 1e-9:
                return  # لا يمكن تحديد الاتجاه إذا كان الخط أو الحركة نقطة
            # --- حساب المتجه العمودي على الخط ---
            normal_x = -line_vec_y
            normal_y = line_vec_x
            norm_len = math.hypot(normal_x, normal_y)
            if norm_len < 1e-9:
                return
            normal_x /= norm_len
            normal_y /= norm_len
            # --- تحديد اتجاه العبور المطلوب ---
            expected_normal_x, expected_normal_y = normal_x, normal_y
            if self.line_direction == 'ltr':
                if normal_x < 0:  # نريد الاتجاه نحو اليمين
                    expected_normal_x, expected_normal_y = -normal_x, -normal_y
            elif self.line_direction == 'rtl':
                if normal_x > 0:  # نريد الاتجاه نحو اليسار
                    expected_normal_x, expected_normal_y = -normal_x, -normal_y
            elif self.line_direction == 'ttb':
                if normal_y < 0:  
                    expected_normal_x, expected_normal_y = -normal_x, -normal_y
            elif self.line_direction == 'btt':
                if normal_y > 0:  
                    expected_normal_x, expected_normal_y = -normal_x, -normal_y
            else:
                return  
            
            dot_product = move_vec_x * expected_normal_x + move_vec_y * expected_normal_y
            crossing_threshold = 0.1 
            print(f"[DEBUG] dot_product={dot_product:.3f}, move=({move_vec_x:.2f},{move_vec_y:.2f}), expected_normal=({expected_normal_x:.2f},{expected_normal_y:.2f})")
            crossed_correctly = dot_product > crossing_threshold
            
            if not hasattr(self, "line_crossed_ids_this_session"):
                self.line_crossed_ids_this_session = set()
            if crossed_correctly:
                if obj_id not in self.line_crossed_ids_this_session:
                    self.line_crossings += 1
                    self.line_crossed_ids_this_session.add(obj_id)
                    print(f"[COUNT - LINE - DotProd] Object crossed ({self.source_name}): obj_id={obj_id}, direction={self.line_direction}, total={self.line_crossings}")
                    bbox_data = getattr(self, 'current_object_bboxes', {}).get(obj_id, {'width': 0, 'height': 0})
                    self.object_crossed.emit(str(self.source_name), str(self.line_direction), int(obj_id), int(self.line_crossings), int(bbox_data['width']), int(bbox_data['height']))
                    self.save_screenshot(frame_to_save,"line_crossing",obj_id)
                else:
                    
                    print(f"[DEBUG - LINE - DotProd] Object {obj_id} already counted. Ignored.")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[ERROR] check_line_crossing (DotProd) failed: {e}")
            import traceback
            traceback.print_exc()   
    def save_screenshot(self,frame,event_type, object_id):
        """ حفظ لقطة الشاشة الحالية"""
        screenshots_dir = self.config.get('screenshots_dir')
        if not screenshots_dir:
            return
        try:
            # إنشاء مجلد فرعي للتاريخ الحالي
            date_folder = os.path.join(screenshots_dir, datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d"))
            os.makedirs(date_folder, exist_ok=True)
            timestamp= datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f") # تصحيح %s إلى %S
            filename=f"{timestamp}_{get_camera_display_name(self.source_name)}_{event_type}_ID{object_id}.jpg"
            filepath=os.path.join(date_folder,filename) # حفظ الصورة داخل المجلد الفرعي
            cv2.imwrite(filepath,cv2.cvtColor(frame,cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR))
            print(f"[SCREENSHOT] Saved screenshot: {filepath}")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[ERROR] Saving screenshot failed: {e}")
            import traceback
            traceback.print_exc()
                   
    def check_region_entry(self, obj_id, p_prev, p_curr,frame_to_save):
        """التحقق مما إذا كان الكائن قد دخل المنطقة المحددة."""
        if not self.scaled_region_rect or not p_curr : return 
        if obj_id in self.region_entered_ids_this_session:
            return
        rx1, ry1, rx2, ry2 = self.scaled_region_rect
        curr_x, curr_y = p_curr
        is_inside = (rx1 <= curr_x < rx2) and (ry1 <= curr_y < ry2)
        if not is_inside: return
        was_outside = True
        if p_prev is not None:
            prev_x, prev_y = p_prev
            was_outside = not ((rx1 <= prev_x < rx2) and (ry1 <= prev_y < ry2))
        if was_outside:
            current_time=time.time()
            if (current_time - self.last_region_entry_time) > self.region_entry_cooldown:
                self.region_crossings += 1
                self.region_entered_ids_this_session.add(obj_id)
                self.last_region_entry_time = current_time # تحديث وقت آخر دخول
                print(f"[COUNT - REGION] Object entered ({self.source_name}): obj_id={obj_id}, total={self.region_crossings}")
                self.save_screenshot(frame_to_save,"region_entry",obj_id)
                # self.object_entered_region.emit(str(self.source_name), int(obj_id), int(self.region_crossings))
                bbox_data = getattr(self, 'current_object_bboxes', {}).get(obj_id, {'width': 0, 'height': 0})
                self.object_crossed.emit(str(self.source_name), "region_entry", int(obj_id), int(self.region_crossings), int(bbox_data['width']), int(bbox_data['height']))
            else:
                  print(f"[DEBUG - REGION] Object {obj_id} entered within cooldown period. Ignored.")
            
    def check_region2_entry(self, obj_id, p_prev, p_curr,frame_to_save):
        """التحقق مما إذا كان الكائن قد دخل المنطقة المحددة 2."""
        if not self.scaled_region2_rect or not p_curr: return
        rx1, ry1, rx2, ry2 = self.scaled_region2_rect
        curr_x, curr_y = p_curr
        is_inside = (rx1 <= curr_x < rx2) and (ry1 <= curr_y < ry2)
        if not is_inside: return
        was_outside = True
        if p_prev is not None:
            prev_x, prev_y = p_prev
            was_outside = not ((rx1 <= prev_x < rx2) and (ry1 <= prev_y < ry2))
        if was_outside:
            # عد الكائن مرة واحدة فقط
            if not hasattr(self, "region2_counted_ids"):
                self.region2_counted_ids = set()
            if obj_id not in self.region2_counted_ids:
                self.region2_crossings += 1
                self.region2_counted_ids.add(obj_id)
                print(f"[COUNT - REGION 2] Object entered ({self.source_name}): obj_id={obj_id}, total={self.region2_crossings}")
                self.save_screenshot(frame_to_save,"region2_entry",obj_id)
                # self.object_entered_region2.emit(str(self.source_name), int(obj_id), int(self.region2_crossings))
                bbox_data = getattr(self, 'current_object_bboxes', {}).get(obj_id, {'width': 0, 'height': 0})
                self.object_crossed.emit(str(self.source_name), "region2_entry", int(obj_id), int(self.region2_crossings), int(bbox_data['width']), int(bbox_data['height']))
            else:
                print(f"[DEBUG - REGION 2] Object {obj_id} already counted. Ignored.")
    def check_polygon_entry(self, obj_id, p_prev, p_curr, frame_to_save):
        if not self.scaled_polygon_points or len(self.scaled_polygon_points) < 3:
            return

        # --- تتبع IDs المحسوبة عالميًا لمنع إعادة العد ---
        # Initialize the global set if it doesn't exist
        if not hasattr(self, 'globally_counted_polygon_ids'):
            self.globally_counted_polygon_ids = set()
        # -------------------------------------------------

        poly = np.array(self.scaled_polygon_points, dtype=np.int32)
        
        # Tolerance margin in pixels - ONLY for already-counted objects to keep zone occupied
        # New counting requires object to be truly inside (dist >= 0)
        POLYGON_TOLERANCE = 15.0  # pixels
        
        try:
            # measureDist=True returns signed distance: +ve (inside), -ve (outside)
            dist = cv2.pointPolygonTest(poly, p_curr, True)
            
            # Strict check: object is truly inside the polygon
            strictly_inside = dist >= 0
            
            # Relaxed check: object is within tolerance margin (for already-counted objects)
            within_tolerance = dist >= -POLYGON_TOLERANCE
            
        except Exception:
            return

        current_time = time.time()

        # CASE 1: Already-counted object - use tolerance to keep zone occupied
        if obj_id in self.globally_counted_polygon_ids:
            if within_tolerance:
                # Object still near the polygon - keep zone occupied
                self.last_time_polygon_occupied = current_time
                if not self.is_polygon_occupied:
                    self.is_polygon_occupied = True
            else:
                # Object has truly left (beyond tolerance)
                if self.is_polygon_occupied:
                    print(f"[DEBUG POLYGON - {self.source_name}] ID {obj_id} (already counted) left polygon. Distance: {dist:.1f}px")
            return

        # CASE 2: New object - must be STRICTLY INSIDE to count
        if not strictly_inside:
            # Debug: Log only if zone is occupied and object is outside
            if self.is_polygon_occupied and dist > -50:  # Only log if close to boundary
                pass  # Removed verbose logging for new objects outside
            return
        
        # Object is strictly inside and not yet counted
        self.last_time_polygon_occupied = current_time

        # Count the object
        if not self.is_polygon_occupied:
            # Zone was empty, now occupied by new object
            self.is_polygon_occupied = True
            self.polygon_crossings += 1
            self.globally_counted_polygon_ids.add(obj_id)
            
            print(f"[COUNT - POLYGON] Zone Occupied by ID {obj_id}. Total: {self.polygon_crossings}")

            try:
                self.save_screenshot(frame_to_save, "polygon_entry", obj_id)
            except Exception:
                pass

            try:
                bbox_data = getattr(self, 'current_object_bboxes', {}).get(obj_id, {'width': 0, 'height': 0})
                self.object_crossed.emit(str(self.source_name), "polygon_entry", int(obj_id), int(self.polygon_crossings), int(bbox_data['width']), int(bbox_data['height']))
            except Exception:
                pass
        
        else:
            # Zone already occupied, but this is a NEW uncounted object inside
            self.polygon_crossings += 1
            self.globally_counted_polygon_ids.add(obj_id)
            
            print(f"[COUNT - POLYGON] New ID {obj_id} entered occupied zone. Total: {self.polygon_crossings}")

            try:
                self.save_screenshot(frame_to_save, "polygon_entry", obj_id)
            except Exception:
                pass

            try:
                bbox_data = getattr(self, 'current_object_bboxes', {}).get(obj_id, {'width': 0, 'height': 0})
                self.object_crossed.emit(str(self.source_name), "polygon_entry", int(obj_id), int(self.polygon_crossings), int(bbox_data['width']), int(bbox_data['height']))
            except Exception:
                pass

    def segment_intersection(self, p1, p2, p3, p4):
        """ التحقق مما إذا كان المقطعان (p1,p2) و (p3,p4) يتقاطعان """
        try:
            p1_f, p2_f, p3_f, p4_f = np.array(p1, dtype=float), np.array(p2, dtype=float), np.array(p3, dtype=float), np.array(p4, dtype=float)
        except Exception: 
            return False
        def orientation(p, q, r):
            val = (q[1] - p[1]) * (r[0] - q[0]) - (q[0] - p[0]) * (r[1] - q[1])
            if abs(val) < 1e-9: return 0  # Collinear (within a small epsilon)
            return 1 if val > 0 else 2 # Clockwise or Counterclockwise
        def on_segment(p, q, r):
            # Check if q lies on segment pr. Use epsilon for float comparisons.
            return (q[0] >= min(p[0], r[0]) - 1e-9 and q[0] <= max(p[0], r[0]) + 1e-9 and
                    q[1] >= min(p[1], r[1]) - 1e-9 and q[1] <= max(p[1], r[1]) + 1e-9)
        o1 = orientation(p1_f, p2_f, p3_f)
        o2 = orientation(p1_f, p2_f, p4_f)
        o3 = orientation(p3_f, p4_f, p1_f)
        o4 = orientation(p3_f, p4_f, p2_f)
        # General case
        if o1 != 0 and o2 != 0 and o3 != 0 and o4 != 0 and (o1 != o2 and o3 != o4):
            return True
        # Special cases (collinear points, one point on segment)
        # p3 is collinear and lies on segment p1p2
        if o1 == 0 and on_segment(p1_f, p3_f, p2_f): return True
        # p4 is collinear and lies on segment p1p2
        if o2 == 0 and on_segment(p1_f, p4_f, p2_f): return True
        # p1 is collinear and lies on segment p3p4
        if o3 == 0 and on_segment(p3_f, p1_f, p4_f): return True
        # p2 is collinear and lies on segment p3p4
        if o4 == 0 and on_segment(p3_f, p2_f, p4_f): return True
        return False
    def stop(self):
        print(f"إشارة الإيقاف للمعالج ({self.source_name}).")
        self.running = False
    # فتحة لتحديث إعدادات الخط أثناء التشغيل (تستقبل من الواجهة)
    @pyqtSlot(object, str, QSize)
    def update_line_settings(self, display_points, direction, display_size):
        print(f"[DEBUG] Processor ({self.source_name}) received updated LINE settings: points={display_points}, direction={direction}, size={display_size}")
        self.initial_display_line_points = display_points
        self.initial_line_direction = direction
        self.display_size = display_size
        self.line_direction = direction # تحديث الاتجاه مباشرة
        self.first_frame_processed = False # إعادة تفعيل حساب النقاط المقاسة
    # --- إضافة: فتحة لتحديث إعدادات المنطقة ---
    @pyqtSlot(object, QSize)
    def update_region_settings(self, display_points, display_size):
        print(f"[DEBUG] Processor ({self.source_name}) received updated REGION settings: points={display_points}, size={display_size}")
        self.initial_display_region_points = display_points
        self.display_size = display_size
        self.first_frame_processed = False # إعادة تفعيل حساب النقاط المقاسة
    @pyqtSlot(object, QSize)
    def update_region2_settings(self, display_points, display_size):
        print(f"[DEBUG] Processor ({self.source_name}) received updated REGION 2 settings: points={display_points}, size={display_size}")
        self.initial_display_region2_points = display_points
        self.display_size = display_size # تحديث الحجم الحالي (قد لا يكون ضروريًا إذا اعتمدنا دائمًا على original_display_size للحساب)
        self.first_frame_processed = False # إعادة تفعيل حساب النقاط المقاسة
    @pyqtSlot(list, QSize)
    def update_polygon_settings(self, display_points, display_size):
        print(f"[DEBUG] Processor ({self.source_name}) received updated POLYGON settings: points={len(display_points) if display_points else 0}, size={display_size}")
        self.initial_display_polygon_points = display_points
        self.display_size = display_size
        self.first_frame_processed = False # إعادة تفعيل حساب النقاط المقاسة

def map_point_to_current_display(p, original_size, current_size):
    """تحويل QPoint من نظام الحجم الأصلي إلى نظام الحجم الحالي للعرض."""
    if not original_size or not current_size or original_size.width() == 0 or original_size.height() == 0:
        return p
    x = int(p.x() * current_size.width() / original_size.width())
    y = int(p.y() * current_size.height() / original_size.height())
    return QPoint(x, y)

        self.counter_font_color = QColor(Qt.white)
        self.counter_bg_color = QColor(0, 0, 0, 128)

        self.populate_camera_list()
    def load_default_config(self):
         # --- تعديل: تحميل الإعدادات الافتراضية من الملف ---
         try:
             with open(SYSTEM_SETTINGS_FILE, 'r') as f:
                 system_settings = json.load(f)
         except (FileNotFoundError, json.JSONDecodeError):
             system_settings = {}
         # ----------------------------------------------------
         # لا نضع line_points/direction هنا، ستأتي من الواجهة أو الملف
         return {
            'min_confidence': 0.55, 'max_iou': 0.6, 'tracking_iou_threshold': 0.75,
            'size_threshold_width': 20, # هذا الإعداد لم يعد يُستخدم بعد إضافة الفلتر الجديد
            'size_threshold_height': 40, # هذا الإعداد لم يعد يُستخدم بعد إضافة الفلتر الجديد
           # 'retracking_window': 15, # إعداد للمتتبع
            'frames_to_skip': 0, # عدد الإطارات لتخطيها
            'target_width': system_settings.get('target_width', 640), # عرض المعالجة المستهدف
            'target_height': system_settings.get('target_height', 384), # ارتفاع المعالجة المستهدف
            'screenshots_dir': system_settings.get('screenshots_dir', os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'screenshots')), # مسار حفظ اللقطات
            'new_setting': 1.0, # <--- تأكد إذا كان هذا الإعداد مستخدمًا بالفعل
            'apply_tta_flip': False, # أو False لتعطيله افتراضيًا
            'tracker_type': 'deepocsort', # <-- إضافة: نوع المتتبع الافتراضي
            'final_nms_conf_threshold': 0.4, # عتبة الثقة لـ NMS النهائي بعد TTA
            'final_nms_iou_threshold': 0.5,  # عتبة IoU لـ NMS النهائي بعد TTA
            'tracker_with_reid': False, # إعداد لإعادة التعرف على الكائنات (ReID)
             'classes': [0],
            'min_box_width_type1': system_settings.get('min_box_width_type1',250),
            'max_box_width_type1':system_settings.get('max_box_width_type1',600),
            'min_box_height_type1':system_settings.get('min_box_height_type1',320),
            'max_box_height_type1':system_settings.get('max_box_height_type1',500),
        }
    def load_appearance_settings(self):
        """تحميل إعدادات المظهر من ملفها الخاص."""
        try:
            with open(APPEARANCE_SETTINGS_FILE, 'r') as f:
                settings = json.load(f)
        except (FileNotFoundError, json.JSONDecodeError):
            settings = {}  # استخدم القيم الافتراضية إذا فشل التحميل

        self.line_color = QColor(settings.get('line_color', '#FF0000'))
        self.region_color = QColor(settings.get('region1_color', '#0000FF'))
        self.region2_color = QColor(settings.get('region2_color', '#FF00FF'))
        self.polygon_color = QColor(settings.get('polygon_color', '#00FF00'))
        self.counter_font_color = QColor(settings.get('counter_font_color', '#FFFFFF'))
        
        # التعامل مع لون الخلفية الشفاف
        bg_color_rgba = settings.get('counter_bg_color', [0, 0, 0, 128])
        self.counter_bg_color = QColor(*bg_color_rgba)

        self.counter_font_size = settings.get('counter_font_size', 12)
        self.zone_thickness = settings.get('zone_thickness', 2)
        print("تم تحميل إعدادات المظهر.")

        main_layout.addLayout(control_layout2, 0)
    def toggle_drawing_region_mode(self, checked):
         self.drawing_region = checked
         if checked:
             # إذا كان وضع رسم الخط مفعلاً، قم بإلغائه
             if self.drawing_line:
                 self.draw_line_button.setChecked(False)
             if self.drawing_polygon:
                 self.draw_polygon_button.setChecked(False)
             self.video_label.setCursor(Qt.CrossCursor)
             print("وضع رسم المنطقة مفعل. انقر واسحب لرسم المستطيل.")
         else:
             self.video_label.unsetCursor()
             self.region_start_point = None # مسح نقطة البداية إذا تم إلغاء الوضع
    def toggle_drawing_mode(self, checked):
        self.drawing_line = checked
        if checked:
            # إذا كان وضع رسم المنطقة مفعلاً، قم بإلغائه
            if self.drawing_region:
                self.draw_region_button.setChecked(False)
            if self.drawing_polygon:
                self.draw_polygon_button.setChecked(False)
            self.video_label.setCursor(Qt.CrossCursor)
            print("وضع رسم الخط مفعل. انقر واسحب لرسم الخط.")
        else:
            self.video_label.unsetCursor()
            self.line_start_point = None # مسح نقطة البداية إذا تم إلغاء الوضع
    def toggle_drawing_region2_mode(self, checked):
        self.drawing_region2 = checked
        if checked:
            # إلغاء تحديد الأزرار الأخرى
            if self.drawing_line: self.draw_line_button.setChecked(False)
            if self.drawing_region: self.draw_region_button.setChecked(False)
            if self.drawing_polygon: self.draw_polygon_button.setChecked(False)
            self.video_label.setCursor(Qt.CrossCursor)
            print("وضع رسم المنطقة 2 مفعل.")
        else:
            self.video_label.unsetCursor()
            self.region2_start_point = None
    def toggle_drawing_polygon_mode(self, checked):
        self.drawing_polygon = checked
        if checked:
            # إلغاء تحديد الأزرار الأخرى
